Motivation: 现有关于群际敌意的社会心理学、道德心理学和政治学理论长期平行发展，对敌意如何形成存在不同甚至冲突的解释，并且很少在真实网络话语中进行比较检验。因此，研究旨在弥合理论传统之间的分歧，明确不同敌意修辞机制在现实话语中的表现及相互关系。

Method: 研究收集2024年美国总统选举期间TikTok、Truth Social和Twitter/X上的286万条帖子，将六种理论机制——边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊化、负面评价、非人化和行动导向——编码或建模到共同的经验分析框架中，并从结构与时间两个维度考察其组织方式和出现顺序。

Result: 结构分析表明，边界建构和威胁建构在敌意修辞系统中具有锚定作用。时间分析显示，边界建构、贬损和行动导向倾向于较早出现；非人化和威胁建构通常较晚出现；替罪羊化出现得最晚。

Conclusion: 论文表明，群际敌意并非单一类别，而是由多个具有结构联系和时间先后的修辞机制组成。该发现为跨社会科学理论整合提供了经验基础，也支持计算社会科学从单标签识别转向对敌意修辞整体机制的建模。

Abstract: Hostile rhetoric toward social groups can normalize exclusion and justify mistreatment, as well as contribute to rising polarization and political violence. Efforts to moderate hostile rhetoric in online speech draw on foundational theories in social and moral psychology, and political science. However, these theories were developed largely in parallel, often propose different and sometimes conflicting accounts of how hostility develops, and have rarely been tested against each other in real discourse. The result is a fragmented understanding of the rhetorical mechanisms of hostility, without a clear sense of how they appear, and relate to each other, in real-world discourse. Using 2.86 million posts from TikTok, Truth Social, and Twitter/X during the 2024 U.S. presidential election, we model the mechanisms of six foundational theories of intergroup hostility -- boundary construction, threat construction, scapegoating, negative evaluation, dehumanization, and action orientation -- within a common empirical framework to recover the broader organization of intergroup hostility rhetoric. Structurally, we find that boundary construction and threat construction anchor the system; temporally, we find that these mechanisms tend to follow a regular ordering: boundary construction, derogation, and action orientation tend to appear early; dehumanization and threat construction later; scapegoating latest. Mapping how these theoretical frameworks actually manifest in discourse bridges longstanding divisions across social science traditions and presents computational social science with a clearer empirical foundation for modeling intergroup hostility rhetoric beyond single-label detection.

</details>


### [161] [Embedding Models Measure in Peculiar Ways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20821)
*Juri Opitz,Andrianos Michail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究词嵌入空间是否能够准确表达质量、距离、时间和体积等物理测量之间客观且唯一的语义相似性与距离。结果表明，嵌入对物理测量关系的建模能力较弱，表现出异常的测量模式，且主要受表面字符串相似性影响；重新校准相似度也未能显著改善其与物理关系的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义嵌入通常用于定义概念之间的相似性和距离，但这些关系未必符合现实世界中的客观结构。质量、距离、时间和体积具有明确的物理测量标准，因此可作为检验嵌入空间是否捕捉真实语义关系的理想对象。本文旨在评估嵌入表示对这类物理测量关系的忠实程度，并识别造成偏差的因素。

Method: 研究者考察词嵌入空间中表示物理测量单位或相关概念的向量关系，将其相似度与物理测量所规定的客观等价关系和距离进行比较。在此基础上，进一步分析嵌入模式是否受到表面字符串相似性的影响，并测试对相似度进行重新校准能否提升嵌入空间与物理测量结构之间的一致性。

Result: 嵌入空间对质量、距离、时间和体积等物理测量关系的建模较弱，未能稳定反映客观的语义等价性和距离。实验中还观察到一些不符合物理直觉的测量模式。进一步分析显示，表示结果显著受到词形或字符串表面相似性的影响；重新校准相似度并没有带来实质性改善。

Conclusion: 常规嵌入空间中的语义距离不能被简单视为现实物理关系的可靠代理。对于具有客观测量结构的概念，嵌入更可能依赖文本表面形式和语料分布，而非真正学习其物理语义关系；仅调整相似度度量不足以解决这一问题。

Abstract: Embedding spaces define notions of semantic similarity and distance. We study whether those embeddings reflect physical measurements of mass, distance, time and volume, which admit a unique, objective notion of semantic equivalence and distance. We find that physical measurement is only weakly modeled in the embedding space, and that instead quite peculiar measurement patterns can be observed. Further analysis indicates that embedding representations of physical measurements are strongly influenced by superficial string similarity, and recalibration of similarity does not substantially improve the alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Polynomial-Time MIMO Detection at the Maximum-Likelihood Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19405)
*Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明：在方形高斯二进制 MIMO 模型中，舍入后的线性 MMSE 估计结合单比特最速下降，可在 O(N^3) 时间内实现精确块恢复，其一阶 SNR 阈值为 ρ=2 log N，与最大似然检测的统计阈值一致。低于约 2 log N 的次一阶范围时，ML 也会失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究方形高斯二进制 MIMO 中精确块恢复的计算—统计差距问题。已有 AMP 状态演化和 replica/MMSE 理论能够描述固定参数下的归一化性能，并预示 2 log N 的尺度，但不能直接保证维度相关高 SNR 区域中的所有坐标同时正确。此前最近的方形系统多项式时间结果——盒约束松弛——的一阶阈值为 4 log N，因此需要构造达到 ML 边界的高效算法，并证明该阈值也是信息论上不可突破的。

Method: 考虑 y=sqrt(ρ/N)Hx*+w，其中 x*∈{±1}^N，H 和 w 为独立标准高斯变量。算法先计算线性 MMSE 估计并进行符号舍入，再执行单比特最速下降。证明分两步展开：首先证明舍入后的估计与真实向量之间仅有次线性数量的汉明错误；其次，对局部搜索可能访问的所有错误集合建立统一的下降性与目标函数障碍界，保证存在可量化降低代价的错误比特，同时搜索路径不会越过障碍，从而最终到达真实解。计算复杂度为 O(N^3) 次单位成本精确实数运算。下界方面，证明当 ρ≤2 log N−log log N−s_N，且 s_N→∞、s_N=o(log N) 时，以真实发送词为中心的某个单比特邻居以高概率具有更优似然，因此 ML 也无法恢复。

Result: 当 ρ≥2 log N 时，算法对任意发送词均以趋于 1 的概率实现精确块恢复，并且复杂度为 O(N^3)。这与穷举 ML 检测的首阶 SNR 阈值相同，也优于此前盒松弛约 4 log N 的首阶阈值。另一方面，在 ρ≤2 log N−log log N−s_N 的次一阶低信噪比区域，ML 以趋于 1 的概率失败，因为存在优于真实词的单比特邻居。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在所采用的精确实数算术模型下，方形高斯二进制 MIMO 的统计精确恢复阈值与多项式时间精确恢复阈值在首阶上重合，消除了该问题上的首阶计算—统计差距。核心贡献是将局部搜索的全路径控制与初始估计的次线性汉明误差结合起来，从而在 ML 边界实现可证明的高效精确恢复。

Abstract: We prove that exact block recovery in the square Gaussian binary MIMO model can be achieved in polynomial time at the same first-order SNR threshold as exhaustive maximum-likelihood detection. Specifically, for $y=\sqrt{ρ/N}Hx^\star+w, \; x^\star\in\{\pm1\}^N,$ and independent standard Gaussian $H\in\mathbb R^{N\times N}$ and $w$, rounded linear MMSE followed by steepest single-bit descent recovers $x^\star$ with failure probability tending to zero, uniformly over every transmitted word and every $ρ\ge2\log N$, using $O(N^3)$ unit-cost exact-real arithmetic operations. The model is a special case of Gaussian random linear estimation, for which AMP state evolution and replica/MMSE formulas rigorously characterize fixed-parameter normalized performance. Those results predict the same $2\log N$ scale, but do not by themselves yield an all-coordinate guarantee in the dimension-dependent regime considered here. To the best of our knowledge, no prior work gives polynomial-time exact block recovery at the ML boundary for this setting; the closest prior square-system theorem, for the box relaxation, has first-order threshold $4\log N$. The proof places the rounded LMMSE estimate at sublinear Hamming distance from the truth, and then establishes, uniformly over every error set the local search can visit, that some wrong bit offers a quantified cost decrease while an objective barrier confines the search path. Conversely, if $0<ρ\le2\log N-\log\log N-s_N$ with $s_N\to\infty$ and $s_N=o(\log N)$, then a one-bit neighbor beats the transmitted word with probability tending to one, so even ML detection fails. Therefore, the statistical and polynomial-time exact-recovery thresholds coincide to first order in the stated arithmetic model.

</details>


### [163] [Binary Deletion Channel Capacity to Within One Hundredth of a Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19412)
*Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对二元删除信道容量长期未知的问题，提出计算机辅助的全参数区间近似方法，构造显式上下界，并将容量估计误差控制在每个传输比特0.0095比特以内；平均认证误差低于0.006522。结果还表明，下界对应的编码速率渐近意义下距真实容量不超过0.019比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元删除信道的精确容量在长期研究后仍未解决。现有工作虽然给出了可达速率和逆定理界，但上下界之间仍存在差距，且需要一种对所有删除概率统一有效、可验证且可复现的精确数值近似方法。

Method: 对于上界，先进行平稳信源约化，再利用覆盖所有允许输入配置的有限不等式。为处理有限窗口之外的未知输入，提出两种构造：其一采用共同的外部幸存序列并控制被省略的删除模式；其二抵消一个熵项，使外部概率以线性形式出现。对于下界，使用有限状态输入，将输出熵估计与若干互不相交的兼容删除掩码计数相结合；独立游程输入则保留输出游程边界的不确定性。通过定向数值检验验证有限不等式，并利用删除概率之间的解析比较，将逐点界推广到整个参数区间。论文同时给出推导、计算成本、完整数值输入及验证程序。

Result: 得到对所有删除概率一致有效的容量近似，其误差小于0.0095比特/传输比特；在删除概率均匀加权下，平均认证误差小于0.006522。估计值取显式下界与上界的中点。下界所对应的编码速率在渐近编码意义下距容量不超过0.019比特。

Conclusion: 该工作显著缩小了二元删除信道容量的上下界差距，并提供了可计算、可验证、可复现的全参数容量近似框架。其核心贡献在于将有限窗口分析、外部输入控制、熵界、删除掩码计数和解析参数延拓结合起来，为进一步逼近精确容量奠定了基础。

Abstract: The exact capacity of the binary deletion channel remains unknown despite decades of work on achievable rates and converse bounds. We establish a computer-assisted approximation whose error is below $0.0095$ bits per transmitted bit, uniformly over all deletion probabilities. The mean certified error bound, with uniform weighting of deletion probability, is below $0.006522$. The estimate is the midpoint of explicit lower and upper bounds. For the converse, a stationary-source reduction is combined with finite inequalities covering every allowed input configuration. Two constructions control the unobserved input beyond a finite window: one uses a common outside survivor sequence and bounds omitted deletion patterns, while the other cancels an entropy term to make outside probabilities enter linearly. For the lower bound, finite-state inputs combine output-entropy estimates with selected disjoint counts of compatible deletion masks; independent-run inputs retain additional uncertainty about output-run boundaries. Directed numerical checks establish the finite inequalities. An analytic comparison between deletion probabilities then extends the pointwise bounds over the entire parameter range. The lower endpoint supplies rates within $0.019$ bits of capacity in the asymptotic coding sense. We give the derivations, recorded computational costs, and complete numerical inputs and programs needed to verify the result.

</details>


### [164] [Source Entropy-Guided Adaptive Transmission for Communication-Driven Multi-View Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19457)
*Mingjie Yang,Guangming Liang,Dongzhu Liu,Lei Zhang,Xiaonan Liu,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种源熵引导的自适应传输框架，用于通信驱动的多视角感知，在有限通信资源和时变信道下协同优化感知采集、数据传输与边缘推理。核心是利用通信间隔刻画数据源熵，并据此在原始数据传输与任务导向传输之间自适应选择；任务导向传输通过ADE-MI实现分布式编码与多视角推理的联合设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信驱动的多视角感知中，通信间隔会影响可获得的源信息，而上行信道状态和通信资源又限制能够上传至边缘服务器的信息量。因此，感知数据获取与边缘推理之间存在耦合关系。现有方法难以同时适应源信息变化、有限比特预算和时变信道，论文旨在解决这一资源受限下的自适应传输与推理问题。

Method: 首先，使用多输出高斯过程建立通信间隔与数据包触发CSI熵之间的关系，并获得解析上界。随后将该熵上界与由传输速率和时延要求决定的可用比特预算进行比较，以选择原始数据传输或任务导向传输。对于任务导向传输，基于信息瓶颈构建通信受限推理问题，并将其分解为自适应分布式编码和多视角推理（ADE-MI），从而避免设备端与边缘服务器之间的交替优化。

Result: 在Widar3.0多视角CSI手势识别数据集上的实验表明：所提出的解析熵上界能够较好地跟随基于正规化流的数值估计；在相同比特预算下，ADE-MI优于任务导向传输基线；在时变信道条件下，源熵引导的自适应框架能够进一步提高识别准确率。摘要未提供具体的数值提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将源信息熵、通信预算和任务推理联合考虑，可以有效缓解通信驱动多视角感知中的感知—传输—推理耦合问题。所提出框架能够根据源信息量和信道条件自适应选择传输模式，并通过ADE-MI提升有限通信资源下的边缘推理性能。

Abstract: Communication-driven multi-view sensing relies on routine communication transmissions for sensing acquisition, while the resulting sensing data at distributed devices must be uploaded to an edge server under limited communication resources. This creates a unique coupling between sensing acquisition and edge inference: the communication interval determines the source information, whereas the uplink condition determines how much information can be delivered to the server for sensing inference. To account for this coupling, we propose a source entropy-guided adaptive transmission framework. Specifically, we characterize the entropy of packet-triggered channel state information (CSI) as a function of the communication interval using a multi-output Gaussian process. The resulting analytical bound is compared with the available bit budget, determined by the transmission rate and latency requirement, to select between original-data and task-oriented transmission. For task-oriented transmission, we formulate the communication-constrained inference problem based on the information bottleneck and decompose it into adaptive distributed encoding and multi-view inference (ADE-MI), which avoids alternating optimization between the devices and the edge server. Experiments on the Widar3.0 multi-view CSI gesture recognition dataset show that the analytical bound closely follows the normalizing-flow numerical estimate, while ADE-MI outperforms task-oriented benchmarks under the same bit budget and the proposed framework further improves recognition accuracy under time-varying channels.

</details>


### [165] [Polynomially larger deletion codes by linear hashing of substring counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19493)
*Eyal En Gad*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明了：二进制码可用冗余3 log₂ n+O(log₂ log₂ n)纠正两次删除；更一般地，可纠正t≥2次删除的码具有(2t−1) log₂ n+O_t(log₂ log₂ n)冗余，改进此前的2t系数上界。核心是随机线性哈希、标签类提取、二染色，以及利用奇环导致编辑重叠来控制剩余冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 删除纠错码的目标是在尽可能低的冗余下恢复被删除的符号。对于两次删除，此前最佳已知上界的主项系数为4，长期未得到改善，而理论上的下界系数为2，存在明显差距。论文旨在降低构造码的冗余，缩小上下界之间的差距；同时将结果推广到任意固定的删除次数t≥2。

Method: 首先对字符串的子串计数进行随机线性哈希，并从哈希标签的一个类别中提取码字，从而比直接构造所需的标签数量减少约n倍。对于仍具有相同标签且彼此混淆的字符串，构造冲突图并进行二染色，以进一步区分冲突对象。论文舍弃含奇环的连通分量；其合理性在于，奇环要求环上的编辑操作发生重叠，而这类结构数量较少，因此丢弃部分只造成可控损失。该组合方法得到两次删除时的3 log₂ n主项，并推广到t次删除。

Result: 对于两次删除，构造出冗余为3 log₂ n+O(log₂ log₂ n)的二进制码，将此前最佳上界的主项系数从4降至3。对于任意固定t≥2，得到冗余(2t−1) log₂ n+O_t(log₂ log₂ n)的码，将此前系数2t改进为2t−1。摘要还指出，随机线性哈希的标签数量相较直接构造减少约n倍，并且奇环相关的冲突分量足够少，不会破坏整体码率。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一种有效的删除纠错码构造框架：通过子串计数哈希进行粗分类，再用图论二染色解决残余混淆，并利用奇环必然导致编辑重叠这一结构性质控制异常情形。该方法显著改进了两次及多次删除纠错码的冗余上界，但与系数2的已知下界仍存在差距，后续研究可继续探索更低冗余或更紧的上下界。

Abstract: We show that binary codes of length $n$ correcting two deletions exist with redundancy $3\log_2n+O(\log_2\log_2n)$. The previous best upper bound had leading coefficient $4$, unchanged since 1965, while the best known lower bound has coefficient $2$. More generally, codes correcting $t\ge2$ deletions exist with redundancy $(2t-1)\log_2n+O_t(\log_2\log_2n)$, improving the coefficient $2t$. We extract a code from one label class of a random linear hash of substring counts, with about $n$ times fewer labels than a direct construction. Confusable words that still share a label are separated by a two-colouring after discarding the words in components with odd cycles, and these are few because an odd cycle forces the edits along it to overlap.

</details>


### [166] [A generalization of the map $χ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19548)
*Xiutao Feng,Qiang Wang,Jingyi Yu,Anpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二元域上的移位不变二次映射，推广了映射 χ_n，并完整刻画其中的置换映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 映射 χ_n 在轻量级密码学中具有重要应用。现有研究聚焦于一个特定形式，因而有必要推广该构造，并系统确定哪些移位不变、代数次数为 2 的映射能够构成置换。

Method: 在有限域 F_2^n 上定义并研究满足移位不变性的二次映射族，利用其循环结构、代数表示及置换条件，对所有相关映射进行分类和完整刻画。摘要未说明所采用的具体定理、算法或证明技术。

Result: 给出了所有移位不变且代数次数为 2 的置换映射的完整刻画，并将经典映射 χ_n 纳入这一更一般的框架。摘要未提供具体分类形式、参数范围或实验数据。

Conclusion: 研究建立了该类二次移位不变置换的完整理论描述，为分析和设计适用于轻量级密码学的非线性映射提供了基础。

Abstract: The mapping $ χ_n:\mathbb{F}_2^n \to \mathbb{F}_2^n$ defined by $y=χ_n(x)$ with $y_i = x_i + x_{i+1}x_{i+2} + x_{i+2}$, where the indices are computed modulo $n$, has been widely studied for its application in lightweight cryptography. In this paper, we generalize this mapping and completely characterize all these shift-invariant permutations of algebraic degree $2$.

</details>


### [167] [Online Material-Labeled Environment Reconstruction via Bayesian Multipath Attribution for Low-Altitude ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19819)
*Meihui Liu,Shu Sun,Ruifeng Gao,Qiuming Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低空融合感知与通信（ISAC）的在线概率框架，将建筑立面建模为虚拟锚点（VA），联合完成VA定位、多径分量（MPC）归属和材料推断，从无线多径观测中构建材料标注的环境地图。该方法通过软归属概率处理不同立面混合的MPC，并结合镜面与漫反射传播效应进行递归贝叶斯融合；射线追踪结果表明，其最终立面材料识别准确率达到93.75%，且保持了较高的立面定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低空ISAC环境重建主要关注几何结构，忽略建筑材料对无线传播的影响，因而难以支持物理一致的信道预测和UAV网络优化。在室外多建筑场景中，从无线多径观测构建材料标注地图还面临三项困难：不同立面的多径分量相互混杂、MPC与具体立面的对应关系不确定，以及UAV sequential measurement under time-varying observation geometries导致观测条件动态变化。因此，需要一种能够在线处理不确定归属、几何估计和材料识别的统一方法。

Method: 方法以每个反射立面为一个虚拟锚点（VA），构建在线统一概率框架。贝叶斯前端估计立面级几何位置，并基于同时考虑主导镜面路径和表面漫反射分量的specular-diffuse likelihood model，计算MPC到各VA的软归属概率。随后，利用这些概率生成归属感知的MPC表示，以VA为中心聚合相关观测，并输入材料推断网络，得到立面级材料证据。最后，通过在线贝叶斯更新递归融合跨时刻证据，输出稳定的材料后验概率和材料标注环境地图。

Result: 在具有代表性的城市街道射线追踪仿真中，所提方法显著优于不进行MPC归属的基线方法；在保留的UAV飞行轨迹上，最终立面级材料识别准确率达到93.75%，同时保持了准确的基于VA的立面定位性能。这表明软MPC归属和在线证据融合能够有效缓解多建筑场景中的路径混合与时变观测问题。

Conclusion: 本文证明，将VA定位、多径归属和材料推断进行联合建模，是从无线多径观测构建传播感知环境地图的有效途径。归属感知表示能够提升材料识别稳定性，specular-diffuse模型增强了对真实表面传播机制的适应性，而递归贝叶斯更新适合处理UAV sequential sensing。总体上，该框架为低空ISAC中的材料感知信道建模和UAV网络规划提供了有潜力的技术基础，但当前结论主要基于仿真场景，实际测量环境中的鲁棒性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Environment reconstruction for low-altitude integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) has largely focused on geometry-centric maps, overlooking material-dependent propagation effects. Material-labeled reconstruction is therefore a key step toward propagation-aware mapping, enabling more physically grounded channel prediction and uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) networking. However, constructing such maps from wireless multipath observations is challenging in outdoor multi-building scenarios because multipath components (MPCs) from different facades are mixed, path-to-facade attribution is uncertain, and UAV measurements arrive sequentially under time-varying observation geometries. To address these challenges, we propose a unified online probabilistic framework that represents each reflecting facade as a virtual anchor (VA) and couples Bayesian VA localization, multipath attribution, and material inference. The Bayesian front end estimates facade-level geometry and computes soft MPC-to-VA attribution probabilities using a speculardiffuse likelihood model, thereby accounting for both dominant specular paths and diffuse surface-interacted components. These attribution probabilities are used to construct attribution-aware MPC representations, which are aggregated in a VA-centric manner and mapped by a material inference network to facadelevel material evidence. The resulting evidence is recursively fused through an online Bayesian update to produce stable material posteriors and material-labeled environment maps. Ray-tracing simulations in a representative urban street scenario show that the proposed method substantially outperforms a no-attribution baseline, achieves 93.75% final facade-level material accuracy on a held-out UAV trajectory, and maintains accurate VA-based facade localization.

</details>


### [168] [Equivalence Between Nested Gibbs Measures and Log-Linear Combinations of Gibbs Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19988)
*Yaiza Bermudez,Samir M. Perlaza,Iñaki Esnaola*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究 Gibbs 概率测度的三种运算：密度幂归一化（重整化）、密度的对数线性组合，以及将一个 Gibbs 测度嵌套为另一个测度的参考测度。后两者均可表述为带相对熵正则项的优化问题，并在特定参数下等价。该等价性被应用于一次性联邦学习，表明聚合本地 Gibbs 算法可达到与集中式汇总数据训练相同的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs 概率测度广泛用于统计学习和带熵正则化的优化，但不同测度运算之间的关系尚未被系统刻画。论文旨在理解重整化、对数线性组合与嵌套操作如何改变 Gibbs 测度，以及它们在优化和联邦学习中的等价性与应用价值。

Method: 定义并分析三种 Gibbs 测度运算：对密度的幂进行归一化、对多个密度进行归一化对数线性组合，以及改变参考测度并将一个测度嵌套于另一个测度中。通过推导其密度形式和对应的变分优化问题，比较不同操作中的目标函数系数与相对熵正则项，并寻找使嵌套与对数线性组合等价的参数条件。进一步将对数线性组合用于一次性联邦学习中的客户端模型聚合。

Result: 研究证明，三种操作产生的结果仍属于 Gibbs 概率测度；其中，对数线性组合和嵌套分别对应于带相对熵正则化的优化问题，二者的差别仅在于目标函数线性组合的系数。存在一组参数，使 Gibbs 测度的嵌套与其对数线性组合完全等价。在联邦学习示例中，服务器对客户端本地训练得到的 Gibbs 算法进行对数线性聚合，其性能与基于所有本地数据集中训练的 Gibbs 算法相同。

Conclusion: 论文建立了 Gibbs 概率测度多种运算之间的统一联系，特别揭示了嵌套与对数线性组合在适当参数下的等价性。这一结果为分布式统计学习提供了理论依据：无需汇总原始数据，仅交换本地 Gibbs 算法即可实现与集中式训练相同的效果。

Abstract: In this paper, three operations on Gibbs probability measures are studied. The first operation, often referred to as renormalization, takes one Gibbs probability measure and generates a new Gibbs measure by normalizing a power of its density. This normalization has a twofold effect: it changes the regularization factor and concentrates the support within a subset of the original support. Interestingly, these effects can be independently controlled by different parameters. The second operation consists of a normalized log-linear combination of the densities of Gibbs probability measures. The third operation takes two Gibbs probability measures and changes the reference measure of the latter with the former. Hence, the former is said to be "nested" within the latter, yielding a new Gibbs probability measure. The resulting measures from the second and third operations are also Gibbs probability measures and are shown, respectively, to solve optimization problems involving the expectations of linear combinations of the objective functions of the given measures, subject to a relative entropy regularization. These optimization problems differ exclusively in the coefficients of the linear combinations. This leads to the conclusion that there exists a set of parameters for which nesting one Gibbs probability measure into another has the same effect as log-linearly combining them. These operations have relevant applications in statistical learning. As an example, a one-shot federated learning system in which clients send their locally trained Gibbs algorithms to the server for log-linear combination is shown to achieve the same performance as a Gibbs algorithm trained upon the aggregation of all local training datasets.

</details>


### [169] [Tri-Hybrid Beamforming Design for Large-Scale MIMO ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20092)
*Tianyu Fang,Mengyuan Ma,Markku Juntti,Inkyu Lee,Joonhyuk Kang,Nhan Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究基于三混合波束成形（tri-HBF）架构的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，通过采用低成本动态超表面天线（DMA）替代传统大规模天线阵列，在通信和感知性能之间进行加权权衡，并以较低复杂度提升能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天线阵列中的传统混合MIMO架构硬件成本和功耗较高，而DMA虽然具有低成本、低功耗优势，但可能带来通信速率与感知性能损失。为兼顾通信吞吐量、感知能力和能效，本文探索适用于ISAC系统的tri-HBF架构及其波束成形设计方法。

Method: 构建一个加权多目标优化问题，联合优化通信和感知性能，即通信总速率与感知总互信息；同时考虑总发射功耗约束以及tri-HBF架构固有的物理限制。针对该非凸问题，利用其结构设计低复杂度迭代算法，并通过闭式更新逐步求解各优化变量。

Result: 数值结果表明，与传统混合MIMO和纯DMA配置相比，所提出的tri-HBF架构能够显著提高能效。在通信总速率和感知总互信息仅发生轻微下降的情况下，实现了更优的能效表现，并在多种系统设置下展现出有效性。

Conclusion: tri-HBF架构能够在ISAC系统中有效平衡通信、感知与能耗之间的关系，是面向大规模天线阵列的高能效实现方案。其优势主要体现在显著降低能耗和提升能效，但需要接受通信速率与感知互信息的小幅性能折衷。

Abstract: Tri-hybrid multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architectures have been proposed as a promising solution for enabling energy-efficient communications systems in large-scale antenna arrays by replacing conventional antenna arrays in hybrid beamforming (HBF) systems with low-cost dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs). In this work, we investigate beamforming design for integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) systems based on tri-HBF architectures, with the objective of jointly enhancing the communications sum rate and sensing sum mutual information. Specifically, we formulate a weighted multi-objective optimization problem that balances communications throughput and sensing mutual information, subject to a total transmit power consumption constraint and the physical limitations inherent to tri-HBF architectures. By exploiting the problem structure, we develop an efficient iterative algorithm with closed-form updates to solve the non-convex optimization problem with low complexity. Numerical results are provided to evaluate the performance of the proposed tri-HBF architecture in various setups. The results demonstrate that the proposed tri-HBF architecture can achieve significant improvement in energy efficiency (EE) compared to the conventional hybrid MIMO and DMA-only configurations, at the cost of minor degradations in sum rate and sensing mutual information.

</details>


### [170] [LEO Satellite Internet of Things: Architecture, Technology, and On-Orbit Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20165)
*Ming Ying,Xiaoming Chen,Qiao Qi,Yichao Xu,Jiajun Pan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向6G物联网的低轨卫星系统架构，并围绕上行、下行和星间链路评估三项关键技术，结合在轨验证平台检验其实用性，最后总结开放问题与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G物联网需要全球覆盖、泛在连接和天地一体化网络能力，传统地面网络难以满足偏远区域覆盖、海量设备接入及高可靠数据传输需求。因此，论文旨在系统化解决低轨卫星与地面网络融合中的架构设计及关键链路技术问题。

Method: 首先构建融合组成视角与功能视角的二维6G低轨卫星物联网系统架构；随后分别针对上行、下行和星间链路研究三项技术：面向上行的海量免授权随机接入、面向下行的基于深度学习的多波束预编码、面向星间链路的分布式协同路由；最后搭建在轨验证平台，对方案的现实可行性和性能进行验证，并归纳未来研究挑战。

Result: 论文对三类关键技术进行了系统评估，并通过在轨验证平台证明所提出解决方案具备实际部署可行性和一定性能有效性。摘要未给出具体实验数据、对比基线或量化增益，因此无法进一步判断各方法的改进幅度。

Conclusion: 低轨卫星能够成为6G物联网的重要基础设施，但其规模化落地依赖于天地融合架构以及接入、传输和路由技术的协同发展。论文为低轨卫星物联网的系统设计和后续研究提供了统一框架，同时指出仍需进一步解决相关开放问题。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are poised to become a cornerstone of the sixth-generation (6G) Internet of Things (IoT), providing truly global coverage and ubiquitous connectivity. This article presents a holistic two-dimensional system architecture for 6G LEO satellite IoT that incorporates composition and functional perspectives to facilitate the seamless integration of LEO satellites and terrestrial networks. Building upon this architecture, we evaluate three pivotal enabling technologies targeting the uplink, downlink, and inter-satellite links (ISLs). Specifically, we analyze massive grant-free random access for efficient uplink connectivity, investigate deep learning-based multibeam precoding for robust downlink transmission, and examine distributed cooperative routing for resilient ISL data delivery. Furthermore, we present an on-orbit verification platform that validates the real-world feasibility and performance of the proposed solutions. Finally, we outline key open challenges and future research directions to guide the realization of future LEO satellite IoT.

</details>


### [171] [Some MDS and ACD codes over commutative non-unital rings of orders 4 and 9 (Revision)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20190)
*Jon-Lark Kim,Marvin Olavides,Young Gun Roe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究有限非单位环 I_2 与 I_3 上的加法互补对偶（ACD）码，建立其与二元、三元 LCD 码的约化对应关系，并据此分类若干短长度下具有最优距离的码；同时给出一般 I_p 情形，并修正此前论文中的若干定理及证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限环上的编码理论可拓展经典有限域线性码的结构与距离理论。I_2 和 I_3 是重要的交换非单位有限环，但其 ACD 码的结构、距离上界及与有限域 LCD 码之间的联系仍不充分明确。此外，既有论文中的部分定理和证明存在需要更正之处，因此有必要重新建立严谨的对应关系并修正相关结论。

Method: 首先利用从 I_2 到 F_2、从 I_3 到 F_3 的约化映射，研究环上 ACD 码与相应有限域上 LCD 码之间的结构关系。随后借助这些关系，分类 I_2 上长度 n=1,2,3 以及部分 n=4,5 情形下具有最大最小距离的 ACD 码，并分类 I_3 上长度 n=1,2 以及部分 n=3 情形下具有最大最小 Lee 距离的 ACD 码。进一步将上述结果推广到一般的 I_p 与 p 元 LCD 码之间的对应。论文还重新检查并修正了原论文定理 3.5、4.7、4.8 及其证明。

Result: 论文建立了 I_2-A​​CD 码与二元 LCD 码、I_3-ACD 码与三元 LCD 码之间的约化关系，并将其推广至 I_p 与 p 元 LCD 码。基于此，获得了若干短长度情形下最优 ACD 码的分类结果：I_2 上覆盖 n=1,2,3 及部分 n=4,5；I_3 上覆盖 n=1,2 及部分 n=3。I_2 情形中，达到最高最小距离的码被证明为 MDS 码。论文同时纠正了先前发表版本中的若干定理表述和证明。

Conclusion: 约化映射为研究非单位有限环上的 ACD 码提供了有效工具，使其结构和距离性质能够借助有限域 LCD 码进行分析。结果表明，I_2 与 I_3 上的部分最优 ACD 码具有良好的经典编码性质，其中 I_2 情形出现 MDS 码。该框架可推广到一般 I_p，但当前分类结果主要限于较短码长，较长长度、完整最优性界限及更一般有限环上的推广仍有进一步研究空间。

Abstract: There are eleven finite rings of order $p^{2}$ denoted by $A_p$ to $K_p$ in alphabetical order. In particular, we consider $I_{2}$ and $I_{3}$ which are commutative non-unital rings of orders 4 and 9 defined by generators and relations as \[I_{p}=\left\langle a,b\mid pa=pb=0,\:a^{2}=b,\:ab=0\right\rangle\] for $p=2, 3,$ respectively. Alahmadi et al. studied codes over these rings. In this paper, we study additive complementary dual (ACD) codes over the rings $I_{2}$ and $I_{3}$. We show relations between ACD codes over $I_{2}$ and binary linear complementary dual (LCD) codes using a reduction map from $I_{2}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{2}$, and between ACD codes over $I_{3}$ and ternary LCD codes using a reduction map from $I_{3}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{3}$. Using the first relation, we classify ACD codes over $I_{2}$ with the highest minimum distances for $n=1, 2, 3$ and partially for $n=4, 5$. It turns out that they are maximum distance separable (MDS) codes. Using the second relation, we classify ACD codes over $I_{3}$ with the highest minimum Lee distances for $n=1, 2$ and partially for $n=3$. We generalize the two relations into a relation between ACD codes over $I_{p}$ and $p$-ary LCD codes using a reduction map from $I_{p}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{p}$.
  This is a correction of the paper published in Advances in Mathematics of Communications, Volume 24, pages 61-76, 2026. In particular, we corrected the statements of Theorems 3.5, 4.7, 4.8, and their proofs.

</details>


### [172] [Conflict-Free Color-Clustered Sequential Belief-Propagation Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20236)
*Mohsen Moradi,Taejoon Kim,Remi A. Chou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向量子LDPC码的无冲突颜色聚类强化学习顺序BP译码器，在基本保持VN级RL-S译码性能的同时，将每轮BP中的调度决策从288个变量节点降至11个颜色类，并支持同色变量节点并行更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码的BP译码虽然复杂度低、时延小，但容易受到短环、简并性和译码收敛失败的影响。已有强化学习顺序BP方法通过学习依赖综合征的变量节点更新顺序来改善性能，但逐变量节点调度限制了单次迭代中的并行度，导致效率受限。因此，本文旨在保留RL-S的自适应调度优势，同时提高更新并行性并避免消息更新冲突。

Method: 首先构建变量节点冲突图：若两个变量节点共享X型或Z型校验节点，则在图中连接。随后对冲突图进行着色，使同一颜色中的变量节点具有互不相交的校验节点邻域，从而避免并行更新之间的消息冲突，并防止来自同一Tanner图4环或6环的变量节点被同时更新。译码过程中，训练好的变量节点级Q表选择一个种子变量节点；之后将其余同色变量节点组成批次，使用相同的预批量消息并行更新。该方法可视为RL-S的颜色聚类扩展。

Result: 在去极化信道下的[[288,12,18]]双变量自行车码实验中，所提出的译码器取得了接近变量节点级RL-S的块错误率性能。同时，每轮BP迭代的调度决策数量由288个变量节点减少至11个颜色类，显著提升了调度效率和潜在并行度。摘要未提供具体错误率数值、复杂度增益或运行时间数据。

Conclusion: 颜色聚类能够有效缓解RL-S逐变量节点调度带来的并行性不足，同时通过冲突图着色保证更新过程无冲突。结果表明，该方法在保持译码性能的基础上大幅压缩调度空间，适合低时延、高并行量子LDPC译码实现。其有效性目前主要由单一[[288,12,18]]双变量自行车码和去极化信道实验支持，泛化到其他码族、信道模型及不同着色策略仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding for quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes is attractive due to its low complexity and low latency, but it is often limited by short cycles, degeneracy, and convergence failures. Reinforcement-learning-based sequential BP decoding (RL-S) improves BP by learning a syndrome-dependent variable-node (VN) update order, but its VN-by-VN schedule has limited within-iteration parallelism. In this paper, we propose a conflict-free color-clustered extension of RL-S. We construct a VN conflict graph in which two VNs are adjacent if they share an X-type or Z-type check, and color this graph so that same-color VNs have disjoint check neighborhoods. This also prevents VNs from the same Tanner 4- or 6-cycle from being updated simultaneously. During decoding, the trained VN-level Q-table selects a seed VN, and all remaining VNs with the same color are updated in parallel using the same pre-batch messages. For the [[288,12,18]] bivariate-bicycle code over the depolarizing channel, our proposed decoder achieves block-error-rate performance close to VN-level RL-S while reducing the scheduling decisions from 288 VNs to 11 color classes per BP iteration.

</details>


### [173] [A Mirror Vanishing Band for Weight Distributions of Binary Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20344)
*Xianmang He*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在Chen–Xie关于二元线性码权重禁区结果的基础上，利用Ashikhmin–Barg极小向量性质中的第三条，证明了关于权重2d的镜像消失带，并改进了码的非零权重数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有Chen–Xie结果表明，在一定维数条件下，权重区间[2d-v, 2d-1]内不存在码字，但其方法只使用了Ashikhmin–Barg关于极小向量的五条基本性质中的两条。本文旨在利用第三条性质进一步研究2d另一侧的权重分布，从而获得新的权重消失区间，并改进非零权重数量的上界。

Method: 针对二元线性[n,k,d]码，利用Ashikhmin–Barg第三条性质，即非极小码字可分解为支集互不相交的码字之和。在假设A_{d+1}=\cdots=A_{d+t}=0（t≥1）且k≥n−2d+1的条件下，分析码字支集分解与最小距离之间的关系，证明权重区间[2d+1,2d+t]中的所有权重系数均为零。随后将该镜像消失带与Chen–Xie给出的[2d−v,2d−1]消失带结合。

Result: 证明了：若A_{d+1}=\cdots=A_{d+t}=0且k≥n−2d+1，则A_w=0对所有w∈[2d+1,2d+t]成立。结合原有消失带后，码的非零权重数至多为n−d−v−2t+1，相比Chen–Xie的上界n−d+1−v减少了2t。

Conclusion: Ashikhmin–Barg关于极小向量的第三条性质能够有效揭示二元线性码在权重2d两侧的对称式禁区结构。新增的镜像消失带不仅扩展了权重分布的理论刻画，也在存在连续初始权重缺失时严格改进了非零权重数量上界。

Abstract: Chen and Xie recently proved, using the Ashikhmin--Barg lemma on minimal vectors, that every binary linear $[n,k,d]$ code with $k=n-2d+2+v$ ($v\ge 0$) has no codewords of weight in the interval $[2d-v,\,2d-1]$. Their argument uses two of the five basic properties of minimal vectors established by Ashikhmin and Barg (1998). In this note we utilize the third property, the disjoint-support decomposition of non-minimal codewords in binary codes, to generate a \emph{mirror} vanishing band on the other side of $2d$: if $A_{d+1}=\cdots=A_{d+t}=0$ for some $t\ge 1$ and $k\ge n-2d+1$, then $A_w=0$ for all $w\in[2d+1,\,2d+t]$. Combining the two bands, the number of nonzero weights of such a code is at most $n-d-v-2t+1$, improving the Chen--Xie bound $n-d+1-v$ by $2t$.

</details>


### [174] [Norm-One Torus Decompositions and Decoding of Gashkov-Sidel'nikov Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20402)
*Minjia Shi,Shitao Li,Yuhong Xia,Tor Helleseth,Ferruh Ozbudak*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究特征为3的有限域上Gashkov-Sidel'nikov循环码与准循环码的综合译码问题，刻画校验列标签集、精确确定陪集重量分布，并构造最大似然译码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果给出了这类码的覆盖半径，但对任意综合如何精确判断最小错误重量，以及如何构造达到该重量的错误向量，仍缺乏完整、统一且可操作的译码方法。本文旨在解决这两个核心问题，并完善循环码与准循环码的译码理论。

Method: 令q=3^m、K=F_{q^2}，利用范数为1的集合T刻画有符号校验列标签。将综合的最小错误重量转化为关于T的最小加法长度，并通过范数与F_q上的二次特征精确判定该长度及完整陪集重量分布。对于重量为3的综合，使用二次特征和与Weil界构造相应的错误向量，从而获得显式译码程序。

Result: 证明循环与准循环Gashkov-Sidel'nikov码的有符号校验列标签集恰为T；依据综合的范数和二次特征精确确定其最小错误重量；得到完整的陪集重量分布，并重新得到覆盖半径为3；同时为每个陪集重量为3的综合构造一个陪集首领。

Conclusion: 本文将该类码的译码问题完整分解并解决：不仅精确知道每个综合对应的最小错误重量，还能构造达到该重量的错误向量。因此所得算法实现了完整的最大似然译码，并统一解释了覆盖半径与陪集结构。

Abstract: Let $q=3^m$, let $K=\mathbb F_{q^2}$, and let \[\mathcal T=\{x\in K^*:\operatorname{N}_{K/\mathbb F_q}(x)=1\}.\] For both cyclic and constacyclic Gashkov-Sidel'nikov codes, we show that the set of signed parity-check column labels is precisely $\mathcal T$. Consequently, the decoding problem separates into two stages: determining the minimum error weight associated with a syndrome $S$ and constructing an error vector attaining this minimum. We identify the former quantity with the minimum additive length of $S$ with respect to $\mathcal T$ and determine it exactly by the norm and the quadratic character of $\mathbb F_q$. We also determine the complete coset-weight distribution and recover the known covering radius $3$. For the constructive part, we use quadratic-character sums and Weil bounds to construct a coset leader for every syndrome of coset weight three. The resulting procedures give complete maximum-likelihood decoders.

</details>


### [175] [Maximum Entropy Probability Distributions on Spheres with Fixed Mean Busemann Function and Holomorphic-Information-Geometric Model of Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20410)
*Vladimir Jacimovic*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究球面上的最大熵概率分布及其信息流形，并将再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中的最小认知努力原则与边界球面上的最大熵原则联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究需要更系统地刻画球面上、尤其是实向量空间和复向量空间中的最大熵分布，并解释其信息几何结构与认知建模之间的关系。论文进一步试图揭示概率分布的最大熵原理与认知过程中的最小努力原则是否存在对偶联系。

Method: 首先，在给定Busemann函数期望值（能量）的约束下，推导实向量空间和复向量空间球面上的最大熵分布族；随后研究复向量空间中规范最大熵分布的信息流形，证明其与Bergman球同构；在此流形上引入再生核，并运用RKHS理论构建认知模型，形式化最小认知努力原则，并分析其与边界球面最大熵原理的关系。

Result: 得到球面上的多类最大熵分布，并识别出一类特殊的规范能级，其逆温度等于球面维数。证明复空间中的规范最大熵分布信息流形同构于Bergman球；同时构造了该流形上的再生核，并展示了RKHS中的最小认知努力原则与边界球面上的最大熵原则之间的对偶关系。

Conclusion: 论文将最大熵统计建模、信息几何和RKHS认知建模统一起来，表明球面上的最大熵分布不仅具有明确的几何结构，而且能够为认知过程提供一种以最小努力为核心的对偶描述。

Abstract: In the first half of the paper we revisit the question regarding MaxEnt probability distributions on spheres. We derive families of MaxEnt distributions on spheres in real and complex vector spaces with fixed expected Busemann function (energy). As a particular case, we deduce sub-families on canonical energy levels where inverse temperature equals the dimension of the sphere. In the second part we focus on the information manifold of canonical MaxEnt distributions on the sphere in the complex vector space. This manifold is isomorphic to the Bergman ball. We introduce the reproducing kernel on this manifold and use the RKHS theory to elaborate the model of cognition. In particular, we state the principle of minimal cognitive effort in RKHS and demonstrate its dual relationship with the MaxEnt principle for probability distributions on the boundary sphere.

</details>


### [176] [Galois Hulls of Generalized Roth-Lempel Codes and Their Applications to EAQECCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20453)
*Xuefei Wu,Qi Liu,Yingchun Chen,Haiyan Zhou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究任意特征有限域上的广义 Roth–Lempel（GRL）码，构造具有预设 Galois hull 维数的 MDS/AMDS 码，并将其应用于纠缠辅助量子纠错码（EAQECC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galois hull 的维数是构造 EAQECC 的重要参数，但在任意特征有限域上系统地构造具有指定 hull 维数的 GRL 码仍具有挑战性。本文旨在提供统一且显式的构造方法，以便同时控制码的 hull 维数、MDS/AMDS 性质及量子码参数。

Method: 作者基于归一化 Lagrange 系数和适当的列乘子，建立统一构造框架；进一步利用乘法陪集、迹纤维、加法子空间陪集以及混合加法—乘法纤维，给出六类显式构造。针对扩域大小 s=2 和 s=3，构造具有特定 Galois hull 维数的 MDS 与 AMDS GRL 码，并将其转化为 EAQECC。

Result: 获得了多类具有预设 Galois hull 维数的 GRL 码，包括 s=2、3 情形下的 MDS 和 AMDS 码。这些码诱导出具有显式维数、纠缠消耗量和较大最小距离的 EAQECC。特别地，零维 hull 的情形给出了 Hermitian LCD GRL 码及相应的最大纠缠 EAQECC。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一构造有效扩展了具有可控 Galois hull 维数的 GRL 码族，并展示了其在 EAQECC 构造中的应用价值。结果兼顾了经典码的最优或近优距离性质与量子码的显式参数，为进一步设计不同纠缠消耗和距离需求的量子纠错码提供了方法基础。

Abstract: The dimension of a Galois hull is an important parameter in the construction of entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes. In this paper, we study generalized Roth-Lempel (GRL) codes with prescribed Galois hull dimensions over finite fields of arbitrary characteristic. We develop a common construction method based on normalized Lagrange coefficients and suitable choices of column multipliers, and obtain six explicit constructions from multiplicative cosets, trace fibers, additive-subspace cosets, and mixed additive--multiplicative fibers. For extension sizes $s=2$ and $s=3$, we construct MDS and AMDS GRL codes with Galois hulls of specific dimensions. The resulting codes provide EAQECCs with explicit dimension, entanglement consumption, and relatively large minimum distance. Taking zero-dimensional hulls also gives Hermitian LCD GRL codes and the corresponding maximally entangled EAQECCs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究强调式时序差分学习（ETD）在恒定步长采样动态中的稳定性问题，并提出正则化强调式TD（RETD）作为一阶后冲击修复方法。作者构造了一个均值映射收缩但采样乘积具有正Lyapunov指数的两状态反例，说明均值稳定不等于样本路径稳定；RETD改变后冲击动态而不改变迹和重要性比率，并在理论与实验上改善稳定性，同时通过读出校正精确恢复ETD固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETD理论主要关注期望更新的稳定性和投影几何，但这两者无法充分刻画恒定步长下实际采样迭代的随机乘积动态。论文旨在揭示均值收缩与样本路径稳定性之间的分离，解释随访迹无限方差与Lyapunov指数符号的关系，并设计一种不改变原有迹及重要性比率、但能修复后冲击动态的稳定化方法。

Method: 首先构造遍历的两状态反例，分析ETD均值映射、采样随机乘积的顶级Lyapunov指数及再生周期，从而将指数符号与随访迹无限方差区分开。随后提出RETD：保留迹和重要性比率，将强调式TD信号存入带泄漏的标量状态，并释放延迟修正。理论上分析RETD的仿射平衡点、单重和双重正则化读出；证明谐波递减步长下的几乎处处收敛，并基于马尔可夫随机乘积界给出条件性的恒定步长矩收缩结果。实验采用成对的10,000次重复运行，在两状态构造和Baird问题上评估稳定性、固定点恢复及任务依赖性。

Result: 两状态反例中，ETD均值映射是收缩的，但采样乘积的顶级Lyapunov指数为正，证明均值稳定不能推出恒定步长样本路径稳定。再生周期分析表明，该正指数并非简单由随访迹的无限方差决定。RETD的原始平衡点相对ETD固定点发生仿射偏移，但单重或双重正则化读出均可精确恢复ETD固定点。RETD在两状态构造和一个Baird设置上获得经理论认证的负Lyapunov指数；实验验证了上述稳定性分离、固定点恢复、非单调稳定区域和任务依赖性。对于正Lyapunov符号的Baird ETD情形，证据仍仅为数值结果。

Conclusion: 论文表明，ETD的期望更新稳定性不足以保证恒定步长采样动态稳定；必须直接分析随机乘积和后冲击行为。RETD能够在不降低共享随访迹方差、也不改变迹和重要性比率的前提下改变后冲击动态，并在一定条件下改善稳定性、保持递减步长收敛及精确恢复ETD目标。不过其效果依赖任务和正则化参数，且部分Baird结论仍缺乏理论证明。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [178] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioPhys-Bridge 是一个面向生物物理学文献的证据驱动科学推理基准，旨在评估语言模型能否将文献证据、定量物理模型与生物学机制可靠连接起来。数据集包含500个案例、1,517项任务，覆盖六个生物学领域和九类物理模型，并通过严格质量控制与专家审核。初步结果显示，DeepSeek-V4-Flash在证据ID F1指标上表现最佳，但整体分数仍偏低，说明复杂科学归因与多步推理仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科生物物理文献分析要求模型同时完成三类工作：从原始文献中定位并引用证据，将观测数据置于定量物理模型中解释，以及把物理结果映射到生物学机制和实验决策。现有通用问答或RAG基准通常缺少稳定证据标识、单位、方程、假设和机制等结构化约束，难以系统评估答案的可追溯性、定量忠实性和科学合理性。因此，论文提出BioPhys-Bridge以填补这一评测缺口。

Method: 论文构建了一个证据驱动的生物物理文献基准。每个案例包含证据块、稳定证据ID、定量值及单位、方程、假设、生物学机制和后续决策等目标信息，可用于问答与检索增强生成评测。数据集首版包含500个案例和1,517个面向智能体的任务，覆盖六个生物学领域、九类物理模型，其中三类稀疏模型作为未来扩展方向。数据构建采用模式检查、证据完整性、定量依据、来源许可、重复样本、单位归一化等质量门槛，并由领域专家审核其中81个案例。模型比较主要采用证据ID F1作为初步指标。

Result: 初步评测中，DeepSeek-V4-Flash取得最高证据ID F1分数0.360，Qwen3.7-Max为0.316，GPT-4o-mini为0.294。结果表明，模型在证据定位和归因方面仍存在明显不足；即使最佳模型的F1也不高，说明面对包含数值、单位、方程、假设和机制的多步骤科学推理任务时，当前模型尚未达到稳定可靠的水平。摘要没有提供其他维度的详细定量结果，因此不能据此判断各模型在物理计算、生物机制解释、幻觉率或实验设计上的相对表现。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge提供了一个面向跨学科科学推理的结构化评测框架，重点考察证据归因、答案忠实性、幻觉抑制以及生物实验设计能力。其主要贡献在于把文献证据与定量物理和生物机制统一到同一基准中。论文认为该数据集可支持更严格的RAG和科学智能体研究，但当前版本规模和模型覆盖仍有限，未来需要扩大数据量与任务复杂度，并开展更全面的评测。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [179] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析2022年1月至2026年8月间提交至arXiv、涉及评测资源的14,767篇论文，考察LLM基准测试在评测对象、任务领域、材料与条件、评分机制及模型参与方式上的演变。研究发现，评测日益关注行动、交互和专业应用；传统与新型设计并存；基于LLM的评分在智能体和非智能体评测中均增长，但模型生成评测材料并未在近期持续增加。论文进一步指出，模型参与构建测试、执行任务和评分，可能削弱评测的独立性并复制模型自身偏好与盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常通过模型排名来描述LLM进展，但排名无法揭示评测标准、任务要求和成功判据本身如何变化。随着基准数量和类型快速扩张，亟需从评测资源的整体演化角度，理解研究界对LLM能力的期待，以及AI参与评测过程后可能带来的独立性与偏差问题。

Method: 作者基于2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv投稿中介绍或更新评测资源的论文，构建包含14,767篇论文的语料库。研究采用分阶段筛选和自动化全文编码，系统分析目标系统与领域、评测材料与实验条件、评分机制，以及LLM在测试材料生成、任务执行和结果评分中的参与方式，并比较不同时间队列及智能体与非智能体评测群体。

Result: 评测重点逐渐转向行动能力、交互能力和专业应用，同时传统设计元素与新兴设计元素并未简单替代，而是经常共存。模型参与呈现不均衡发展：基于LLM的评分在智能体和非智能体评测中均有所增长；相比之下，模型生成评测材料在较新的论文队列中没有表现出同等程度的持续增长。

Conclusion: LLM评测研究正在把不断扩展的能力期待转化为更复杂的任务、条件与成功标准。AI同时参与测试构建、任务完成和回答判定，虽可能提高评测规模与适应性，却也带来评测独立性下降的风险，并可能重现参与模型的偏好、偏差和盲点。因此，未来应关注评测的来源独立性、判定可靠性和模型参与所造成的系统性偏差。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [180] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张为复合智能体系统构建基础模型操作系统（FMOS），以统一抽象和管理模型交互、状态、记忆、资源、验证与策略执行，从而提升可移植性、可信性和治理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI应用正从单一基础模型转向由多个模型、工具和智能体组成的复合系统。尽管MCP、A2A等协议改善了连接性，但不同框架仍内置各自的运行时机制，导致状态、记忆、预算和安全护栏难以迁移，系统行为不可移植，治理脆弱。作者借鉴操作系统出现前的软件生态，认为领域缺少一个统一的系统抽象层。

Method: 本文采用立场论证而非实证研究方法。作者提出FMOS概念，将其类比为操作系统或虚拟机：对上层应用提供仿佛独占、可信且能力近乎无限的基础模型实例；对下层则负责多层记忆中的知识编排、模型选择、资源分配、验证与策略执行，并根据运行经验持续调整干预策略，在直接推理与额外控制之间动态切换。

Result: 本文未报告具体实验、数据集、基准或定量结果。其主要产出是一个概念性系统架构与研究议程：通过FMOS统一运行时能力，期望实现跨框架行为可移植、资源使用更高效、模型交互更可靠，以及更稳健的安全与治理。

Conclusion: 作者认为，随着复合智能体系统成为主流，FMOS将成为类似传统操作系统的重要基础设施。它应屏蔽底层模型与运行时复杂性，同时集中承担记忆、调度、验证和政策治理职能，以支撑可信、可持续和可扩展的AI应用生态。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [181] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 TranSGrid，用统一任务同时评估演绎、归纳和溯因推理下的系统泛化。七个 Transformer 在 TranSGrid 上显著低于普通留出测试集，且仅恢复任一现有简化都会使性能回升，说明现有基准低估了归纳与溯因需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统泛化要求模型将已知的原子元素重新组合以解决新问题，但现有研究常采用近似线性的动作组合、基于生产性的测试或动作显式的目标等简化设置。这些设置便于控制实验，却可能削弱系统泛化中的关键推理要求。因此，论文旨在识别这些简化所掩盖的能力，并建立更全面、严格的评估框架。

Method: 作者提出 TranSGrid 测试平台，将演绎推理、归纳推理和溯因推理整合到统一任务中，并在 4,800 个实例上评估七个 Transformer。实验首先比较模型在 TranSGrid 与留出测试集上的表现；随后构造两个变体：一是使动作近似线性组合，以降低归纳需求；二是使目标变得动作显式，以降低溯因需求。通过这些对照实验分析不同任务简化对系统泛化评估的影响。

Result: 所有模型在 TranSGrid 上的表现都明显低于留出测试集。最大模型在留出测试集上的准确率为 79.6%，在完整 TranSGrid 上降至 55.3%，在最困难子集上仅为 15.8%。即使测试长度处于训练长度范围内，性能差距仍然存在，表明仅依赖生产性或长度外推不足以衡量系统泛化。两个简化变体的求解率均恢复到大致接近普通测试集的水平，说明单独加入任一简化就足以显著降低任务难度。

Conclusion: 现有系统泛化任务往往降低了归纳需求、溯因需求，或同时降低二者，因此可能将复杂的系统泛化退化为普通的留出测试。全面评估系统泛化需要同时保留演绎、归纳和溯因三类推理要求；TranSGrid 为此提供了一个统一测试框架。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [182] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了对话式大语言模型智能体开展网络搜索的完整流程，涵盖是否搜索、如何构造查询、搜索结果来源偏好以及如何基于结果生成回答。研究结合真实用户交互与API控制实验，发现不同平台的搜索决策和查询策略存在显著差异；搜索频率更高并不必然带来更高回答质量；搜索结果存在平台特定的域名偏好；部分回答声明虽源于搜索结果却未进行引用，影响可追溯性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式大语言模型越来越依赖网络搜索，智能体搜索从决策、查询、结果获取到回答生成的端到端生命周期仍缺乏系统理解。现有研究不足以解释不同平台如何决定是否搜索、如何组织查询、是否偏向特定信息来源，以及搜索结果如何被转化为最终回答。因此，本文旨在全面评估对话式智能体的网络搜索行为及其质量、可靠性与归因问题。

Method: 研究对象包括ChatGPT、Claude、Grok和DeepSeek四个主要对话平台。作者结合两类实验：一是基于真实用户交互的实地研究（invivo），二是使用相应平台模型API开展的受控实验（invitro）。研究围绕四个环节展开：网络搜索调用决策、搜索查询构造策略、搜索结果中的来源域名偏好，以及基于搜索结果生成有依据回答时的选择与引用行为。

Result: 研究发现：1）不同平台和模型在是否调用网络搜索方面差异显著；2）更高的搜索调用频率并不一定对应更好的回答质量；3）智能体采用多样且复杂的查询构造策略；4）不同平台的搜索引擎会返回具有平台特定偏好的来源域名；5）最终回答整体上较多依赖搜索结果，但仍有部分陈述来自未被引用的搜索结果，造成来源归因不完整，并带来可靠性和可验证性风险。

Conclusion: 本文首次较系统地刻画了对话式智能体网络搜索的端到端生命周期。研究表明，优化智能体搜索不能仅关注搜索次数，还应同时改进搜索触发决策、查询规划、来源多样性、结果筛选和细粒度引用机制。对于未来的对话式检索系统，应加强搜索行为的可解释性、信息来源的透明度以及回答陈述与证据之间的准确对齐。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [183] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoTuring，通过在相同加速器优化空间中比较具备架构语义的智能体与处理匿名变量的盲搜索智能体，测量“理解硬件含义”本身对设计优化的贡献。初步结果显示，语义信息显著提升性能与样本效率，但结构化批评机制可替代大部分语义优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体硬件设计评估通常只证明设计性能有所提升，却无法判断智能体是否真正理解机器，还是仅仅在无须理解参数含义的情况下有效搜索。由于既有研究固定了问题表述方式，无法将架构推理能力与一般优化或搜索能力区分开来。本文旨在构造一个能够直接测量“问题是否具有架构语义”影响的对照实验。

Method: 提出 AutoTuring，在同一个15维加速器设计空间上使用同一个智能体进行两种条件的优化：第一种提供带名称的架构旋钮和模拟器计数器，第二种将变量匿名化并归一化到[0,1]。两种条件保持评估器、合法设计空间和可达最优解完全一致，因此主要差异是问题表述是否具有可解释的架构语义。实验在包含9个内核的FP16 GEMM测试集上进行，并进一步加入批评循环，以检验结构化批评能否弥补盲搜索的差距。

Result: 在平均结果上，具备架构语义的智能体相较于建模H200提升5.4%，相较于盲智能体提升12.3%，同时模拟器调用次数减少70.1%。加入批评循环后，盲智能体恢复了大部分性能差距，而对具备架构语义的智能体几乎没有额外收益，表明架构知识与结构化批评在该实验中更像替代关系，而非互补关系。作者强调结果仅来自单一建模加速器下每个条件5至6次运行，属于初步发现。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献不是提出一个更快的加速器，而是提出一种通过控制变量区分架构理解与一般搜索能力的评估框架。结果初步表明，赋予智能体有意义的架构表述可提升优化质量和效率，但高质量的结构化批评也能替代相当一部分这种优势。由于实验规模较小、硬件模型单一且重复次数有限，关于可迁移性和普适性的结论仍需更多架构、任务和运行次数验证。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [184] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MAGS：一个多智能体代码生成与形式验证框架，通过 Dafny 将生成程序转换为可验证表示，利用验证器反馈自动修复，并编译回可执行代码；在 220 个跨领域任务上均生成了满足冻结安全规范且具有非平凡安全保证的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编程智能体能够生成规模和复杂度不断增加的程序，使人工审查难以全面覆盖，从而增加安全与安保失效风险。模糊测试、静态分析和 LLM 验证器虽能发现部分问题，但难以穷尽边界情况。传统形式化验证能够提供机器可检查的安全保证，却通常依赖大量人工编写规格和证明工程。因此，论文旨在降低形式化验证的使用门槛，并将其融入 LLM 代码生成流程。

Method: 提出统一的多智能体框架 MAGS。首先冻结经过人工审计的 API 与安全需求；随后将 LLM 生成的代码转换为 Dafny 这一具备验证感知能力的中间表示；利用 Dafny 验证器检查形式化安全性质，并根据验证反馈自动修复违反规格的代码；最后将通过验证的程序编译回可执行代码。框架在 CUDA 内核、终端脚本和机械臂任务三个领域进行评估，共 220 个样例。

Result: 在 100 个 CUDA 内核、100 个终端脚本和 20 个机械臂任务上，MAGS 对全部 220 个样例均成功生成了满足冻结规格、且具有非平凡安全保证的程序，形式验证成功率达到 100%。独立的安全性与功能性评估表明，该框架在三个领域总体表现较强。但实验也发现：当自动形式化得到的语义无法完整表达目标行为时，系统仍会出现失败。

Conclusion: MAGS 表明，将多智能体代码生成、Dafny 中间表示和验证器反馈闭环结合，可以有效为生成式程序提供机器可检查的形式安全保证，并显著减少人工证明工程负担。不过，100% 的结果主要针对预先冻结的形式化规格，并不等同于程序在真实环境中的绝对安全。系统的关键限制在于规格和语义建模质量：若形式化需求不完整或存在偏差，验证结果可能无法覆盖真实功能与安全风险。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [185] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究工具增强型大语言模型智能体的工具幻觉问题，提出五类单注册表幻觉分类和五类MCP多服务器幻觉分类，给出训练无关的闭世界解析器作为基准，并通过大规模测评证明幻觉检测必须先于工具权限门控。研究还发布可复现的HTB基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择与工具安全方法通常默认模型调用的工具真实存在、参数符合某个工具模式，因此无法处理虚构工具、未声明参数以及多服务器合并后的名称冲突和遮蔽问题。本文旨在揭示这一结构性盲点，明确工具幻觉防御在工具门控链路中的优先级，并建立统一、可比较的测量基准。

Method: 首先建立工具幻觉五分类体系H1-H5；随后提出训练无关的闭世界Resolution Rung解析器，通过工具注册表成员关系和签名/schema检查验证调用。论文从形式上论证幻觉防御必须位于因果权限门控之前，并刻画无法进一步消除的残余情形，即参数借用后在schema层面与合法调用不可区分。实验覆盖10个托管模型、两种工具调用界面，并比较原始JSON等不同约束条件。进一步针对Model Context Protocol的多服务器命名空间合并，提出M1-M5分类并在真实MCP环境中测量。最终发布版本化Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark（HTB）。

Result: 在单注册表场景中共测得322次真实工具幻觉；虚构工具调用在约束较弱的原始JSON界面上明显更多，达到34次，而另一界面仅3次。模型规模未带来明显改善，675B模型的表现可与7—8B模型相当。在MCP场景中测得154次幻觉，且一些在单注册表测试中表现正常的前沿模型也会出错，原因在于多服务器合并引入了单一注册表无法表达的名称冲突和遮蔽结构。论文还识别出schema不可区分的借用参数这一不可约残余。

Conclusion: 工具幻觉不是普通的工具选择错误，而是工具调用链路中的前置结构性问题：若调用对象本身不存在，后续权限门控就无法有效拒绝。因此，系统应先进行注册表存在性与签名/schema解析，再执行工具选择、权限和安全策略。MCP的多服务器合并进一步扩大了攻击面和测量维度。HTB为未来解析器和防御方法提供了统一评测基础，但仅靠解析器无法解决所有schema等价的语义歧义。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [186] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于目标表征（goal representations）的综述与理论分析，主张区分目标的形式与意义，并将不同目标表示视为一个可比较的设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学与计算机科学通常把目标视为组织目的性行为的认知状态，但既有研究往往默认目标是固定的行为约束，较少系统考察目标表示本身的表达能力、设计特性、效率及其对行为的影响。本文借鉴语言学和逻辑学中句法—语义接口的思想，旨在澄清目标作为表征的性质，并揭示不同目标概念背后的隐含假设。

Method: 本文采用综合性理论综述方法，整合认知科学、计算机科学、语言学和逻辑学中关于目标表示的研究。作者围绕目标表示的形式与内容展开分析，考察不同目标语言的表达能力、行为刻画范围，以及不同表示是否能够对应相同的行为，并据此构建目标表示的设计空间。

Result: 文章指出，目标是具有组合性的表征，其内容能够与世界知识灵活结合，从而组织和规定合理的目的性行为。不同目标表示在表达能力、设计结构和计算效率上存在差异；目标语言是否足以表达相关行为，以及不同表示是否具有行为等价性，是评价目标表示的重要维度。区分目标的形式与意义，还有助于分析高层认知与动机之间的关系。

Conclusion: 作者认为，目标不应仅被视为施加行为约束的固定状态，也应被研究为具有不同形式、语义和计算性质的表征系统。将目标置于一个更广泛的设计空间中，有助于揭示目标理论的隐含前提，比较不同目标观念，并进一步理解高层认知、动机和理性行为之间的联系。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>


### [187] [Compositional Reasoning in Language Models under Reinforcement Learning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19465)
*Yu He,Yingxi Li,Yifei Wang,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习后训练对语言模型组合推理能力的影响，提出依赖图框架并发现显著的“分解到组合”迁移不对称：在分解任务上训练不一定能迁移到组合任务，而在组合任务上训练更容易迁移回分解任务。该现象在长度外推、结构分布偏移、未见技能迁移及初步工具调用任务中得到评估或支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合推理是现实问题求解的基础，但训练数据有限，模型需要将已学技能以新方式组合。尽管强化学习等后训练方法提升了语言模型的推理能力，其对组合泛化的影响仍缺乏系统理解。本文旨在形式化组合推理、揭示不同复杂度任务之间的迁移规律，并解释分解任务与组合任务之间的泛化不对称性。

Method: 提出基于依赖图的组合推理框架，将组合性划分为三个递增复杂度层级。选用数据结构任务作为实验载体，以获得确定性的奖励计算和清晰的组合结构；比较分解技能训练与组合任务训练之间的迁移效果。进一步在长度外推、结构分布偏移、未见技能迁移等设置下评估组合泛化，并在真实工具调用基准上开展初步验证。理论上对观察到的迁移不对称进行解释。

Result: 实验持续观察到分解到组合的不对称现象：仅在分解技能上训练，不能稳定迁移到组合任务；而在组合任务上训练，通常更容易迁移回分解任务。该现象在长度外推、结构分布偏移和涉及未见技能的迁移场景中得到进一步考察；真实工具调用基准的初步结果也显示类似趋势可能存在于实际应用中。

Conclusion: 强化学习后训练提升推理能力并不意味着能够自然获得组合泛化能力。组合任务训练可能包含更丰富的结构化交互信号，因此比单独的分解技能训练更有利于学习可迁移的组合推理能力。依赖图框架为分析这一现象提供了形式化工具，也提示后训练数据和任务设计应直接覆盖目标组合结构，而不能只依赖基础技能的拼接。

Abstract: Compositional reasoning is critical for real-world problem solving: since training data is necessarily limited, models must generalize by composing learned skills in new ways. While post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) have substantially improved the reasoning abilities of language models (LMs), their effects on compositional reasoning remain less well understood. We propose a dependency-graph framework to formalize compositional reasoning, yielding three levels of compositionality with increasing complexity. Empirically, we instantiate this framework with data-structure tasks, which provide deterministic reward computation and clear compositional structure. We find a consistent decomposed-to-composed asymmetry: decomposed-skill training does not reliably transfer to composed tasks, whereas composed-task training transfers more readily back to decomposed tasks. We provide theoretical explanation for this asymmetry, and further evaluate compositional generalization under length extrapolation, structural distribution shift, and transfer to tasks requiring unseen skills. Finally, we present a pilot study on real-world tool-calling benchmarks, showing preliminary evidence that the decomposed-to-composed asymmetry can extend to practical settings.

</details>


### [188] [Safety Beyond the Interface: Detecting Harm via Latent States in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19472)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Chiquita Prabhu,Omkar Neogi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究利用大语言模型内部激活训练轻量级探针，以低延迟、低计算成本识别有害提示词，性能接近大型外部安全模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部护栏模型无法访问目标语言模型的内部状态，且会带来显著延迟与计算开销，在资源受限和时间敏感的自主系统中限制了实用性。论文试图验证：语言模型内部是否已经编码了其对输入有害性的判断，从而通过内部表征实现更高效的安全检测。

Method: 从 LLaMA-3.1-8B 提取内部激活，并训练包含 1260 万参数的轻量级多层感知机（MLP）分类探针，用于检测有害提示词。在 WildJailbreak、Beavertails 和 AEGIS 2.0 三个数据集上进行评估，并以 F1 分数及相对大型护栏模型的延迟、计算成本和参数规模进行比较。

Result: 探针在 WildJailbreak、Beavertails 和 AEGIS 2.0 上分别取得 99%、83% 和 84% 的 F1 分数。其效果可与参数规模大约高 1000 倍的护栏模型竞争，同时显著降低延迟和计算成本。结果表明，目标模型的内部激活确实包含可用于识别有害提示词的有效信息。

Conclusion: 研究证明，基于模型内部激活的轻量级安全探针能够在保持较高检测性能的同时降低安全基础设施的开销，为自主系统部署低延迟、资源高效的安全监控机制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) yet the safety infrastructure surrounding these models introduces latency and compute overhead. This limits utility in resource-constrained, time-critical deployments. Existing external guardrail models remain blind to the model's internal workings, creating a fundamental assurance gap. We ask: does the model already know when the content is harmful? We extract activations from LLaMA-3.1-8B and train lightweight MLP classifier probes (12.6M parameters) to detect harmful prompts. Evaluated on WildJailbreak, Beavertails, and AEGIS 2.0, our probes achieve F1 scores of 99%, 83%, and 84%, respectively competitive with 1000x larger guard models while cutting latency and compute costs.

</details>


### [189] [QVAC Genesis III: A Large-Scale, High-Quality Open Synthetic STEM Corpus for Efficient Language Model Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19513)
*Davide Vitabile,N. Ranjan,Akshay Nambiar,Kamal K. Gupta,Amril Nazir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出QVAC Genesis III，一个面向小型边缘语言模型的191.43B-token STEM多领域合成预训练语料库，并通过双重生成策略提升单位token的信息价值；在1.7B模型从零训练消融实验中，其效果优于Cosmopedia-v2和Cosmo-1B，在ARC、GPQA Diamond及MMLU STEM上取得显著提升，且有效答案率最高达99.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向教育和STEM场景的边缘AI与端侧部署，小模型受限于参数规模和token预算，对高质量、高信息密度预训练数据有更强需求。现有开放生态缺少专门针对STEM、兼顾多领域、多难度和教育风格的高效合成数据集，因此需要构建能够提升小模型学习效率的公开语料库。

Method: 构建QVAC Genesis III，规模为191.43B tokens，覆盖19个STEM相关领域、多个难度等级和不同教育风格。采用双重生成策略进行定向教师蒸馏：以弱边缘级学生模型作为反馈信号，将学生失败样本转化为纠错性解释；将学生成功样本扩展为针对所有答案选项的对比式、选项级推理。实验使用1.7B参数模型从零开始训练，并与Cosmopedia-v2及公开Cosmo-1B进行受控对比和消融。评估时引入LLM-as-a-parser协议，从自由形式输出中提取最终答案，同时统计准确率与答案有效性。

Result: 在ARC、GPQA Diamond和MMLU STEM基准上，使用QVAC Genesis III训练的模型 consistently 优于Cosmopedia-v2训练模型及Cosmo-1B。相对基线最高提升包括ARC-E 28.57%、ARC-C 21.35%；有效答案率最高达到99.45%。结果表明，该语料库能够提高小模型在STEM任务上的学习效率、答案正确性和输出可解析性。

Conclusion: QVAC Genesis III验证了利用弱学生模型反馈生成纠错解释与对比式选项推理，可以构建高单位token价值的STEM合成预训练数据。该数据集对资源受限的小型语言模型和端侧教育应用具有潜在价值。不过，摘要未说明数据生成成本、潜在合成偏差、数据污染控制、各领域的独立贡献以及与更强数据配方或模型规模的比较，这些因素仍需在完整论文中进一步验证。

Abstract: High-quality pre-training data is a critical bottleneck for educational and STEM-specific language models targeting edge AI and on-device deployment where token budgets are tightly constrained. While major organizations train ever-larger models on private corpora, the open ecosystem lacks STEM-focused synthetic datasets that deliver high per-token learning value efficiently for small models. To address this gap, we introduce QVAC Genesis III, a 191.43B-token, STEM-focused multi-domain synthetic corpus covering 19 domains across several difficulty levels and different educational styles. QVAC Genesis III is built via a dual generation strategy that performs targeted teacher distillation using a weak edge-scale student model as signal: the student's failures are converted into corrective explanations, while its successes are expanded into contrastive option-level reasoning over all answer choices. We further introduce an LLM-as-a-parser evaluation protocol that extracts final answers from free-form outputs and tracks both accuracy and answer validity. To validate the effectiveness of our QVAC Genesis III data, we conduct controlled from-scratch ablations with 1.7B-parameter models, showing that models trained with QVAC Genesis III consistently outperform both models trained with the open-source synthetic corpus Cosmopedia-v2 and the publicly released Cosmo-1B model across ARC, GPQA Diamond, and MMLU STEM benchmarks, achieving up to +28.57% on ARC-E and +21.35% on ARC-C, while reaching a Valid Answer Rate of up to 99.45%.

</details>


### [190] [LLM-as-an-Improver: Turning Verification into Better Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19515)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VRR（Verify–Repair–Reselect）框架，将验证器从单纯的候选排序工具扩展为候选改进器。方法保留初始最优解，并基于验证反馈生成修复最优解、修复次优解和采用新思路的三类候选，过滤无效与重复候选后重新选择最终答案。在代码生成和推理基准、多种模型上的实验表明，VRR通常优于固定候选池的验证器选择方法，甚至能在初始候选全部错误时恢复正确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于验证器的选择方法通常只在固定候选池中进行排序，验证反馈在候选评估结束后被丢弃，因此无法进一步改善候选集合。本文旨在探究验证反馈能否用于构造更优的新候选，从而突破初始候选池的限制，并提升最终解答质量。

Method: 提出LLM-as-an-Improver思想和VRR框架。首先保留初始候选中的获胜解；随后有条件地生成三类互补候选：对获胜解的修复版本、对次优解的修复版本，以及基于新解题思路生成的候选。方法仅利用推理阶段信息过滤无效和重复候选，最后按照原有评估标准对扩展后的候选集合重新选择最终答案。

Result: 在多种语言模型、代码生成任务和推理基准上，VRR在许多设置下优于固定候选池的验证器选择方法。实验还显示，即使初始候选池中的所有解都不正确，VRR也能够通过验证反馈生成并恢复正确解。

Conclusion: 验证器的作用不应局限于在已有解之间排序；其反馈还可以驱动候选解的修复、重构与扩展。LLM可以作为候选改进器，在初始候选集合之外构造更强解答，从而提升验证器选择框架的能力和适用范围。

Abstract: Verifier-based selection improves LLM performance by generating multiple candidate solutions and using a verifier to select the most promising one. However, existing methods typically treat verification only as a ranking step and discard its feedback once a fixed candidate pool has been evaluated. In this paper, we ask whether verification can also improve the candidate set itself. To this end, we introduce LLM-as-an-Improver and propose Verify--Repair--Reselect (VRR), which uses verification feedback to generate and reselect improved candidates. VRR retains the initial winner while conditionally generating three complementary alternatives: repaired versions of the winner and runner-up, and a solution based on a new approach. It filters invalid and duplicate candidates using only inference-time information and then reselects the final answer under the original evaluation criteria. Across diverse models and code-generation and reasoning benchmarks, VRR improves over fixed-pool verifier-based selection in many settings and can recover correct solutions even when all candidates in the initial pool are incorrect. These results highlight a broader role for LLMs as improvers: verification feedback can not only select among existing solutions but also construct stronger candidates beyond the initial pool.

</details>


### [191] [An Architecture for Long-Horizon Agents: Levels, Ticks and Cascaded Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19519)
*Erik Nijkamp,Anurag Koul,Egor Pakhomov,Bo Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向长期任务的持续运行智能体架构。核心观点是：智能体必须先具备跨上下文、跨进程和跨时间持续运行且不遗忘的基础，才可能实现持续学习。架构包括多时间尺度层级摘要、定时自主行动单元，以及失败后逐级升级模型的级联智能。十天强化学习复现实验证明，该系统能保持任务连续性、利用外部记录改变后续行为，并为未来引入学习模块提供位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体通常受限于上下文窗口、进程生命周期和人工关注间隔，难以执行持续数天或数周的复杂任务。论文旨在解决长期任务中的记忆保持、持续运行、自主调度、资源控制和持续学习承载等问题，并强调这些能力主要依赖模型外围的系统架构，而非单纯依赖模型参数更新。

Method: 提出由三部分组成的分层架构：（1）按时间尺度组织的多级结构，每一级维护一个有界文件，用于概括下一级的状态和工作内容；（2）以带时钟的tick作为自主行动的基本单位，使智能体能够持续、周期性地执行任务；（3）级联智能机制，只有在较弱模型或当前处理流程评审失败后，才将任务升级给能力更强的模型。通过该架构开展为期十天的实验，让智能体在每天仅有一次人工关注的条件下复现一项已发表的强化学习结果。

Result: 实验显示：（1）智能体在多次上下文重置和会话边界后仍保持了整个任务脉络；（2）早期写入的操作知识能够在不更新模型权重的情况下改变后续行为；（3）系统中的检查、评审和运行流程为未来嵌入可学习组件提供了明确位置。该结果初步支持了外部持久化运行基底对长期智能体的重要性。

Conclusion: 长期智能体的持续学习不能仅依靠模型本身，而需要一个寿命超过所有上下文和进程的外部运行基底。论文进一步认为，智能体已有的检查、评审和调度机制是加入学习能力的合适位置；先解决持续运行和不遗忘问题，是实现可靠持续学习的前提。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out work spanning days or weeks, such as an operations remediation or a research programme. Such a task outlives any context window, any process and any interval at which a person can attend. In this paper, we argue that a long-horizon agent must run continually without forgetting before it can learn continually. This ability lies in the harness around the model rather than in the model itself. We derive seven bottlenecks from the long-horizon setting and answer them with a hierarchical architecture of three parts: (i) levels indexed by time scale, each keeping a bounded file summarising the level below; (ii) a clocked tick as the unit of autonomous action; and (iii) cascaded intelligence, where work is escalated to a more capable model only after failing review. We report on a ten-day campaign in which an agent built on this architecture reproduced a published reinforcement-learning result with a human attending once a day, and show (1) the agent kept the thread across every context reset and session boundary of the campaign, (2) operating knowledge written early changed later behaviour with no change to model weights, and (3) where learned components would enter such a system. Overall, our experience suggests continual learning for these agents needs a substrate outliving every context and process, and the checks the harness already runs are where a learner belongs.

</details>


### [192] [EconSkills: Studying Skill Transfer and Retrieval for Web Agents on Live Economic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19523)
*Yinzhu Quan,Zefang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EconSkills，一个将已验证的经济数据网页操作轨迹抽象为可复用标准操作流程（SOP）的技能库与评测框架，用于研究网页代理在新任务中的程序迁移与技能库选择能力。结果表明，参数化技能可提升已覆盖任务的成功率并减少步骤，但在全库场景中，未覆盖任务上的近似匹配会抵消部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页代理评测通常丢弃先前成功交互中获得的操作程序，无法衡量程序复用、跨任务迁移和持续积累能力。由于经济数据网站具有重复访问、站点特定导航和动态数据验证等特点，有必要构建能够保存并复用程序知识的技能库，同时区分“已知相关程序能否迁移”和“代理能否从技能库中正确选择并利用技能”这两个问题。

Method: 基于 EconWebArena 的已验证网页交互轨迹构建 EconSkills。每项技能包含适用范围、导航流程、站点特定指导、验证检查和恢复步骤，并将具体数据值替换为占位符，以形成参数化、可跨实例复用的标准操作流程。实验比较无技能提示、原始轨迹回放和抽象技能提示，并分别开展受控迁移评测与技能库规模评测；同时依据浏览器轨迹分析导航效率和语义验证需求。

Result: 在受控迁移实验中，匹配的抽象技能相较无技能提示提高任务成功率，并在成功任务中减少操作步骤；抽象技能明显优于直接回放原始轨迹。在完整技能库场景中，整体检索效果与无技能基线具有竞争力，并在技能直接覆盖的任务上表现最好。但对于未覆盖任务，近似匹配带来的错误或低效会抵消部分收益。轨迹分析显示，程序指导能够缩短站点特定导航，但语义层面的结果验证仍不可完全省略。

Conclusion: 可复用的经济网页操作程序能够跨任务实例迁移，并为网页代理的长期技能积累提供了可行路径。未来重点应放在覆盖感知的技能选择、近似匹配控制以及更有效的上下文传递机制，以避免技能库扩展后错误检索削弱性能。

Abstract: Web agents often revisit the same sites, yet most evaluations discard the procedures learned in earlier successful interactions. We introduce EconSkills, a skill library and evaluation framework that distills verified EconWebArena trajectories into parameterized standard operating procedures for retrieving live economic data. Each skill records its scope, navigation procedure, site-specific guidance, verification checks, and recovery steps while replacing source-instance values with placeholders. EconSkills separates two questions: whether a known relevant procedure transfers to a held-out task, and whether an agent can retain that benefit when selecting from a library. In controlled transfer, matched skills improve success over no-skill prompting and require fewer steps on paired successes, while abstraction is substantially more effective than replaying raw trajectories. At library scale, retrieval is competitive with the no-skill baseline overall and performs best on directly covered tasks; coverage-stratified outcomes show that approximate matches on uncovered tasks offset these gains. Browser trajectories further identify when procedural guidance shortens portal-specific navigation and when semantic verification remains necessary. These results establish that reusable economic web procedures can transfer across task instances and provide a concrete design target for coverage-aware selection and context delivery.

</details>


### [193] [A Unified Evaluation Framework for Trustworthy Large Language Models, Agentic AI, and Multimodal Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19524)
*Shaina Raza,Ahmed Y. Radwan,Imran Liaquat,Kathryn Hume*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的人工智能可信度评估框架，覆盖LLM、智能体系统和多模态模型，通过八个可信维度、分层评估、证据追踪和元评估，提升评估结果的可解释性、可比性与治理适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准分数无法完整反映现代人工智能系统的可信度。不同类型系统在能力、交互轨迹和跨模态行为方面需要不同评估方式，而开发、监管和监督又要求评估证据具备可解释性、可追溯性和可复现性。此外，单一综合分数可能掩盖安全关键缺陷，因此需要一个既保留系统特定指标、又支持跨系统比较的统一框架。

Method: 构建统一评估框架，将输出级、轨迹级和跨模态评估连接起来，并从能力、鲁棒性、安全、公平、透明度、治理、监督和效率八个维度刻画系统。框架保留原生评估指标，同时将其映射到共同的性能等级，并附带不确定性估计和可追溯证据。进一步引入元评估层，检验评估过程本身的有效性、可靠性和可复现性；利用多维画像展示优势与弱点，设置安全关键覆盖规则，避免聚合分数掩盖严重问题；同时将评估结果映射至治理框架、国际标准和欧盟监管要求。

Result: 框架能够在统一结构下整合不同人工智能系统及不同层次的评估证据，形成多维可信度画像，并同时评价系统性能与评估证据的可信程度。它支持跨系统的共同性能等级比较、不确定性表达、证据追踪以及与治理和监管要求的衔接。摘要未提供具体实验数据或定量结果，但指出该框架已面向不同部署情境提出验证方向。

Conclusion: 该研究为现代人工智能系统的可信度评估提供了结构化、可解释且面向治理的基础。其核心贡献不是用单一分数替代现有指标，而是在保留系统差异的同时建立统一的比较和监督机制。未来关键工作是开展跨部署场景的实证验证，以检验框架的实际有效性、稳定性和可推广性。

Abstract: Benchmark scores alone provide an incomplete basis for assessing the trustworthiness of modern artificial intelligence systems. Large language models (LLMs), agentic systems, and multimodal models (MLLMs) require different forms of assessment, yet their evaluation evidence must remain interpretable for development and oversight. We propose a unified framework that connects output-level, trajectory-level, and cross-modal assessment through eight trustworthiness dimensions: capability, robustness, safety, fairness, transparency, governance, oversight, and efficiency. The framework preserves system-specific metrics while mapping native measurements to common performance bands, accompanied by uncertainty estimates and traceable evidence. A meta-evaluation layer examines the validity, reliability, and reproducibility of the evaluation itself. Multidimensional profiles expose strengths and weaknesses, while safety-critical overrides prevent aggregate scores from masking critical failures. Mappings to governance frameworks, international standards, and European Union regulatory requirements connect technical assessment with oversight needs. The framework provides a structured basis for assessing both system performance and the credibility of the evidence supporting it, with empirical validation across deployment contexts remaining an essential next step.

</details>


### [194] [Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19526)
*Xinghong Fu,Aravinth Kulanthaivelu,Yutaro Yamada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SIFT，一种面向代码智能体的高效递归自我改进框架，通过 LLM 成对评审、正则化 Bradley–Terry 模型和轻量级树搜索，在严格计算预算下提升代码生成性能，并减少 CPU 时间、墙钟时间与 API 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体自我进化方法通常通过重新运行大量基准任务来评估候选自修改的有效性，导致候选评估成为主要运行时瓶颈，计算成本和时间开销都很高。因此，论文旨在设计一种样本高效、资源受限条件下仍能有效引导搜索的自我改进方法。

Method: 提出 Recursive Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search（SIFT）。该方法将昂贵的下游任务评估与 LLM-as-a-judge 的成对比较结合：评审模型比较候选补丁，利用胜负记录通过正则化 Bradley–Terry 模型估计候选强度；强度分数进一步用于轻量级、解耦式树搜索中的基于排名的父节点采样。只有最有潜力的节点才接受昂贵的下游任务评估，从而利用中间评审信号引导搜索并降低评估瓶颈。

Result: 在完整 Polyglot 基准上，SIFT 超越了现有基于树搜索的自我进化框架，同时显著降低了 CPU 小时数、墙钟时间和 API 成本。摘要未提供具体的性能提升百分比或绝对资源数值。

Conclusion: SIFT 表明，使用 LLM 成对评审作为低成本中间反馈，并结合统计建模与解耦树搜索，可以在严格预算下实现有效的代码智能体递归自我改进。该框架在保持或提升编码性能的同时，显著改善了资源效率。

Abstract: Coding agents can recursively modify their own implementations, forming a loop of self-improvement. While prior work shows this can boost performance on coding benchmarks, existing approaches are costly and compute-intensive.
